## 🔥 News

🧰 **2026-09 · Open-Source Workbenches section.** Platforms you can actually clone and run now have their own home.

🤗 **2026-09 · Hugging Face Daily Papers links.** Every entry whose paper has a 🤗 Daily Papers page now carries a direct badge, so you can jump straight to the community discussion and upvotes.

🚀 **2026-08 · Repository launch.** First release covering AI Scientist systems, co-scientists, benchmarks, datasets, and scientific environments.

💡 **Ongoing · PRs welcome.** Missing something strong? Open a pull request. One excellent entry beats five weak ones.

---

## 🤖 End-to-End AI Scientists

Systems that connect several research stages into one loop. Human supervision is still normal, and "autonomous" is not treated as a binary claim.

- [OmniScientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558), "An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist". ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2608.13558-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Omni-Scientist/OmniScientist?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/Omni-Scientist/OmniScientist) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2608.13558&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2608.13558) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://omni-scientist.github.io/)

### ⚠️ License traps worth knowing

**AI-Researcher** (`HKUDS/AI-Researcher`, 5.7k stars) ships substantial code but has **no license file at all**, so all rights are reserved.
**Coscientist** (`gomesgroup/coscientist`) carries a **Commons Clause** rider forbidding sale, which is not OSI open source.
**The AI Scientist** and **v2** use a custom AI Scientist Source Code License derived from the Responsible AI license, carrying use restrictions.
**UniScientist** (`UniPat-AI/UniScientist`) has no license file.

## 📊 Benchmarks & Evaluation

Evaluation is the bottleneck. These test scientific knowledge, literature grounding, code and experiment execution, whole research workflows, or interaction with scientific environments.

## 📦 Datasets, Corpora & Environments

Corpora, tools, simulated worlds, and domain data that agents feed on. Scoped to scientific-agent use, not an exhaustive AI-for-Science catalog.

### Agent environments and research corpora

- [DiscoveryWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06769), "A Virtual Environment for Developing and Evaluating Automated Scientific Discovery Agents". ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2406.06769-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/allenai/discoveryworld?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/allenai/discoveryworld)
- [ScienceWorld](https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.07540), "Is your Agent Smarter than a 5th Grader?". ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2203.07540-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/allenai/ScienceWorld?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/allenai/ScienceWorld) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2203.07540&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2203.07540)
- [ScholarQABench](https://github.com/AkariAsai/ScholarQABench), Benchmark and data for scientific question answering over scholarly literature. ![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/AkariAsai/ScholarQABench?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)
- [HypoGen](https://huggingface.co/datasets/UniverseTBD/hypogen-dr1), Structured scientific hypothesis data released with Sparks of Science. ![Dataset](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Dataset-06B6D4?style=flat-square)
- [S2ORC](https://allenai.org/data/s2orc), Large open scientific paper corpus for scholarly NLP and retrieval. ![Data](https://img.shields.io/badge/Data-2EA44F?style=flat-square)
- [OpenAlex](https://openalex.org/), Open scholarly graph and API over works, authors, institutions, and concepts. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white) [![Docs](https://img.shields.io/badge/Docs-0EA5E9?style=flat-square)](https://docs.openalex.org/)

### Scientific data and tools

- [Materials Project](https://materialsproject.org/), Computed materials properties, structures, and discovery infrastructure. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ChEMBL](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chembl/), Curated bioactivity database for drug-discovery research. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [PubChem](https://pubchem.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/), Open chemical information and compound database. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ProteinGym](https://proteingym.org/), Protein fitness prediction datasets and evaluation resources. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

### 📄 Scholarly corpora & literature APIs

- [peS2o](https://github.com/allenai/pes2o), Cleaned 40M-document S2ORC derivative built specifically for language-model pretraining on scientific full text. ![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/allenai/pes2o?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717) [![Dataset](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Dataset-06B6D4?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/allenai/peS2o) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=huggingface&logoColor=black)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/allenai/peS2o)
- [unarXive 2022](https://doi.org/10.1109/jcdl57899.2023.00020), "All arXiv Publications Pre-Processed for NLP, Including Structured Full-Text and Citation Network". ![JCDL 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/JCDL_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/IllDepence/unarXive?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/IllDepence/unarXive) [![Dataset](https://img.shields.io/badge/%F0%9F%A4%97%20Dataset-06B6D4?style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/datasets/saier/unarXive_citrec)
- [arXiv bulk data](https://info.arxiv.org/help/bulk_data/index.html), Official requester-pays S3 bulk access to full-text sources and metadata for the entire arXiv corpus. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [CORE](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41597-023-02208-w), "A Global Aggregation Service for Open Access Papers". ![Scientific Data 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Scientific_Data_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://core.ac.uk/)
- [Semantic Scholar Academic Graph API](https://api.semanticscholar.org/), Programmatic access to 200M+ papers with citation contexts, embeddings, author records and paper recommendations. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [PMC Open Access Subset](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/tools/openftlist/), Bulk full-text XML for millions of openly licensed biomedical articles from PubMed Central. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [bioRxiv and medRxiv API](https://api.biorxiv.org/), JSON endpoints for preprint metadata, full-text links and published-version tracking across both preprint servers. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [OpenReview API v2](https://docs.openreview.net/), Programmatic access to submissions, reviews, rebuttals and decisions across major machine-learning venues. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [Papers with Code successor](https://huggingface.co/papers), Papers with Code has shut down; its domain now redirects to Hugging Face Papers, the de facto successor index. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

- [SPICE](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41597-022-01882-6), "SPICE, A Dataset of Drug-like Molecules and Peptides for Training Machine Learning Potentials". ![Scientific Data 2023](https://img.shields.io/badge/Scientific_Data_2023-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/openmm/spice-dataset?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/openmm/spice-dataset)
- [OQMD](https://oqmd.org/), Over one million DFT-computed thermodynamic and structural properties of inorganic compounds with API access. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [AFLOW](https://aflowlib.org/), Automatic-flow repository of millions of computed materials entries exposed through a REST interface. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NOMAD](https://nomad-lab.eu/nomad-lab/), FAIR repository and analysis platform for raw and processed computational materials data across simulation codes. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [JARVIS](https://jarvis.nist.gov/), NIST-hosted integration of DFT, machine-learning and experimental materials datasets plus public leaderboards. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [Materials Cloud](https://www.materialscloud.org/), Open platform for reproducible computational materials science with archived AiiDA provenance graphs. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [RRUFF](https://rruff.info/), Reference Raman, X-ray diffraction and chemistry data for well-characterized mineral specimens. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [QCArchive](https://qcarchive.molssi.org/), MolSSI public quantum chemistry results archive with a Python client for constructing large QC datasets. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [CrystaLLM training data](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-024-54639-7), "Crystal structure generation with autoregressive large language modeling". ![Nature Communications 2024](https://img.shields.io/badge/Nat_Commun_2024-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![Paper](https://img.shields.io/badge/Paper-DOI-006633?style=flat-square&logo=doi&logoColor=white) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/lantunes/CrystaLLM?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/lantunes/CrystaLLM)

### 🧬 Biology & biomedicine

- [UniProt](https://www.uniprot.org/), Curated protein sequence and functional annotation knowledgebase exposed through a documented REST API. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [RCSB PDB](https://www.rcsb.org/), Experimental three-dimensional biomolecular structures with programmatic search and data web APIs. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [AlphaFold Protein Structure Database](https://alphafold.ebi.ac.uk/), Over 200M predicted protein structures with per-residue confidence scores and free bulk download. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NCBI GEO](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/geo/), Public functional genomics repository of curated expression series, platforms and individual samples. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ENCODE](https://www.encodeproject.org/), Uniformly processed functional genomics assays across human and mouse, served through a REST API. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NCBI SRA](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/sra), Archive of raw high-throughput sequencing reads across organisms and study types. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [European Nucleotide Archive](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/ena/browser/home), EMBL-EBI nucleotide sequence archive mirroring and complementing SRA with its own browser and API. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [CELLxGENE Discover](https://cellxgene.cziscience.com/), Standardized single-cell corpus of tens of millions of cells with an API and in-browser exploration. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [PRIDE](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/pride/), Proteomics identifications and mass spectrometry raw data repository hosted at EMBL-EBI. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [protocols.io](https://www.protocols.io/), Machine-readable wet-lab protocol repository, directly useful as a grounding source for protocol-planning agents. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

### 🔭 Physics, astronomy & earth

- [CERN Open Data](https://opendata.cern.ch/), Petabytes of LHC collision and simulated data released with virtual machines and runnable analysis examples. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [GWOSC](https://gwosc.org/), Gravitational-wave strain data and event catalogs from the LIGO, Virgo and KAGRA observatories. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [MAST](https://archive.stsci.edu/), Space Telescope Science Institute multi-mission archive for Hubble, JWST, TESS and Kepler with programmatic access. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NASA ADS](https://ui.adsabs.harvard.edu/), Astrophysics literature and citation database with a full API, the standard discovery layer for astronomy. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [SDSS SkyServer](https://skyserver.sdss.org/), Sloan Digital Sky Survey imaging and spectra queryable directly through SQL. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NASA Exoplanet Archive](https://exoplanetarchive.ipac.caltech.edu/), Curated exoplanet and host-star parameter tables with TAP query access and bulk downloads. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ESGF](https://esgf-node.llnl.gov/), Federated distribution network for CMIP climate model output and related earth-system simulation data. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [NASA Earthdata](https://www.earthdata.nasa.gov/), Earth-observation data portal spanning NASA distributed active archive centers and their APIs. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [The Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00568), "a Large-Scale Collection of Diverse Physics Simulations for Machine Learning". ![NeurIPS 2024 Datasets and Benchmarks](https://img.shields.io/badge/NeurIPS_2024_D%26B-4B5563?style=flat-square) ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2412.00568-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/PolymathicAI/the_well?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/PolymathicAI/the_well) [![Daily Papers](https://img.shields.io/badge/dynamic/json?url=https%3A%2F%2Fhuggingface.co%2Fapi%2Fpapers%2F2412.00568&query=%24.upvotes&label=%F0%9F%A4%97%20Daily&color=FFD21E&style=flat-square)](https://huggingface.co/papers/2412.00568) [![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://polymathic-ai.org/)

### 🏗️ Research-data infrastructure

- [Zenodo](https://zenodo.org/), CERN-operated DOI-issuing general repository hosting a large share of long-tail scientific artifacts and code snapshots. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [OSF](https://osf.io/), Open Science Framework project registry and repository covering preregistrations, materials and data. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [Harvard Dataverse](https://dataverse.harvard.edu/), Large general-purpose research data repository issuing DOIs, widely used across social and life sciences. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [ICPSR](https://www.icpsr.umich.edu/web/pages/), Primary social-science data archive holding curated survey, administrative and longitudinal study collections. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)
- [GESIS](https://data.gesis.org/), German social-science data archive and infrastructure for survey and computational social science data. ![Website](https://img.shields.io/badge/Website-2EA44F?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)

## 🎓 Tutorials, Blogs & Talks

Technical reports and announcements from the labs actually building these systems.

### 📰 Blogs & technical reports

- [Google DeepMind, AlphaEvolve](https://deepmind.google/blog/alphaevolve-a-gemini-powered-coding-agent-for-designing-advanced-algorithms/), Gemini-powered coding agent that discovers new algorithms and improves known bounds. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind, AlphaEvolve one year on](https://deepmind.google/blog/alphaevolve-impact/), Deployed impact across genomics, power grids, and quantum computing. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind, Co-Scientist](https://deepmind.google/blog/co-scientist-a-multi-agent-ai-partner-to-accelerate-research/), Multi-agent research partner, the lab write-up behind the Nature publication. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind, AlphaTensor](https://deepmind.google/blog/discovering-novel-algorithms-with-alphatensor/), Reinforcement learning discovering faster matrix multiplication algorithms. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepMind-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=deepmind&logoColor=white)
- [Google, Gemini for Science](https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/research/gemini-for-science-io-2026/), Hypothesis generation, computational discovery, literature insights, and science skills. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Google-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=google&logoColor=white)
- [Google Research, AI co-scientist](https://research.google/blog/accelerating-scientific-breakthroughs-with-an-ai-co-scientist/), Official overview of the multi-agent hypothesis engine. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Google-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=google&logoColor=white)
- [Google DeepMind and Isomorphic Labs, AlphaFold 3](https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/products/google-deepmind-isomorphic-alphafold-3-ai-model/), Structure prediction extended to proteins, nucleic acids, ligands, and ions. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Google-Blog-4285F4?style=flat-square&logo=google&logoColor=white)
- [Anthropic, Claude Science](https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-science-ai-workbench), An AI workbench for scientists, the closed reference point for open workbench efforts. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Anthropic-Blog-D97757?style=flat-square&logo=anthropic&logoColor=white)
- [Anthropic, Claude for Life Sciences](https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-for-life-sciences), Connectors for Benchling, PubMed, and 10x, plus science-specific agent skills. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Anthropic-Blog-D97757?style=flat-square&logo=anthropic&logoColor=white)
- [Anthropic, AI for Science Program](https://www.anthropic.com/news/ai-for-science-program), Free API credits for researchers running scientific workloads. ![Blog](https://img.shields.io/badge/Anthropic-Blog-D97757?style=flat-square&logo=anthropic&logoColor=white)
- [Meta FAIR, OMol25 and fairchem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08762), "The Open Molecules 2025 (OMol25) Dataset, Evaluations, and Models". ![arXiv](https://img.shields.io/badge/arXiv-2505.08762-B31B1B?style=flat-square) [![Code](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/facebookresearch/fairchem?style=flat-square&logo=github&label=Code&color=181717)](https://github.com/facebookresearch/fairchem)

## Contributing

Focused additions, corrections, and better primary links are all welcome. Read [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md) first.

A strong entry has at least one of these.

- a peer-reviewed paper or a technically substantive preprint;
- a public implementation, benchmark, dataset, environment, or reproducible artifact;
- an evaluation measuring scientific validity, reproducibility, tool use, or research productivity;
- a clear tie to literature review, ideation, experiment design, experimentation, analysis, or writing.

Prefer one excellent entry over several weak ones. Generic chat wrappers, unsupported "AI Scientist" marketing, and papers with no scientific-agent relevance are out of scope.
